# Oteryn Game / Canary parity inventory — appendix: Shared-source difference ledger

Appendix of [the parity inventory](OTERYN_GAME_CANARY_PARITY_INVENTORY_2026-10-25.md). Status semantics and limits are defined there.

## 8. Shared-source difference ledger

This appendix lists every differing paired Canary Lua file (same relative path within paired data packs) and every differing C++ opcode parser body. It records both pins and one example per pair; the untracked JSON ledger contains all changed non-comment source lines for follow-up. A body can differ in more places than the example. This is an exhaustive **source-surface inventory**, not an exhaustive semantic proof for arbitrary Lua or runtime configuration. All unproven subbranches retain PARTIAL status. Removed/added numeric and quoted-string summaries below are complete multisets; changed branch header lines are also listed. These record messages/constants/predicates, not full third-party implementations. Multiset counts preserve repeated constants; unchanged constants used in a different expression still require the cited body comparison.

| Packet parser | Canary | Crystal | Executable source example | Literal and branch differences (both sources) | Oteryn parity qualification |
